Model before rewriting:

letting b be 3
letting k be 3
letting l be 20
letting r be 60
letting v be 3
find bibd: matrix indexed by [int(1..v), int(1..b)] of int(0..1)
find x: int(-5..5)
find y: int(-5..5)

such that

bibd[bibd[x, y], bibd[x, y]] = 1

   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
